## 1620062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、お主が攻めてくるとはな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>運命神は、つくづくわしを飽きさせん。<end>"
EN: "And of all people, <value:$28>, you are the one attacking me. The God of Fate never allows me to grow bored.<end>"
KO: "게다가 네가 쳐들어오다니, <value:$28>. 운명의 신은 정말 날 지루하게 두지 않는군.<end>"

## 1620063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス…、<line-break>そうか、お主がおったのか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お主ならば、あの敗軍を<line-break>まとめる器量がある！<line-break>くっ、ぬかったわ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... Of course! You were here! You have the ability to rally that defeated army. Damn it, I miscalculated!<end>"
KO: "제네테스…. 그렇군, 네놈이 있었나! 네놈이라면 그 패잔군을 수습할 역량이 있다! 큭, 방심했군!<end>"

## 1620064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がおるとわかっておれば、<line-break>敗残兵の殲滅を優先させたものを！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Had I known you were here, I would have made exterminating the survivors my first priority!<end>"
KO: "네놈이 있는 줄 알았다면 패잔병 섬멸을 우선했을 것을!<end>"

## 1620065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とっつぁん、<line-break>悪いが、命をもらう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールの兵の<line-break>命がかかっているんでな！<end>"
EN: "Sorry, old man, but I'm taking your life. The lives of Rostorl's soldiers depend on it!<end>"
KO: "…아저씨, 미안하지만 목숨을 받겠다. 로스톨 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있어서 말이야!<end>"

## 1620066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、そうですかと<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかんな。<line-break>　<line-break>こっちも<line-break>ディンガルの兵の命が<line-break>かかっておるのでな！<end>"
EN: "I cannot simply hand it over because you ask. The lives of Dyneskal's soldiers depend on me as well!<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇습니까 하고 순순히 내줄 수는 없지. 이쪽도 딘갈 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있으니까!<end>"

## 1620067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>他は任せた！！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, handle the rest!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나머지는 맡긴다!!<end>"

## 1620068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン将軍！！<end>"
EN: "General Angeerdan!<end>"
KO: "앙길단 장군!!<end>"

## 1620069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ……、<line-break>運命の女神は残酷なものよ。<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<line-break>ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed. To abandon me after I had come so far... Xenetes... To think a man of your caliber was heir to... House Pharos... Urgh...<end>"
KO: "후후……, 운명의 여신은 잔혹하군. 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니…. 제네테스여…. 너 정도의 사내가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽….<end>"

## 1620070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みなに伝えよ！<line-break>バラバラに地下王国を通るのだ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Tell everyone! Pass through the Underground Kingdom separately! All of you... must survive...!<end>"
KO: "모두에게 전해라! 흩어져 지하왕국을 통과하는 거다! 모두… 살아남아라…!<end>"

## 1620071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょ、将軍！！<line-break>…くっ！<end>"
EN: "G-General! Ngh!<end>"
KO: "자, 장군님!! …큭!<end>"

## 1620072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "撤退！　撤退せよ！！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>各部隊に伝令！<line-break>将軍が討ち取られた！<line-break>すみやかに撤退を開始せよ！！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! All forces, retreat! Send word to every unit! The general has fallen! Begin the retreat at once!<end>"
KO: "철수! 철수하라!! 각 부대에 전령! 장군이 전사했다! 즉시 철수를 개시하라!!<end>"

## 1620073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵の略奪部隊の場所がわかりました！<end>"
EN: "We've located the enemy's raiding party!<end>"
KO: "적 약탈 부대의 위치를 알아냈습니다!<end>"

## 1620074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、ここを切り上げて、<line-break>そっちを片付けに移動する。<line-break>部隊をまとめ直すぞ！<end>"
EN: "Good. We're done here. We'll move out and deal with them next. Regroup the troops!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 여기 일은 마무리하고 저쪽을 처리하러 간다. 부대를 다시 정비해!<end>"

## 1620075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes, sir!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1620076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1620077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…じゃあな、とっつぁん。<line-break>あの世で、いつか飲み明かそうぜ。<end>"
EN: "See you, old man. Someday, in the next world, we'll drink till dawn.<end>"
KO: "…잘 가라, 영감. 저세상에서 언젠가 밤새 술이나 마시자고.<end>"

## 1620078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、アンギルダンが<line-break>討ち取られると、帝国軍は潰走した。<end>"
EN: "After Angeerdan was slain in a surprise attack by Xenetes, who had rallied the defeated soldiers with astonishing speed, the Imperial Army fled in disarray.<end>"
KO: "패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 무너져 도주했다.<end>"

## 1620079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで、ゼネテスの声望は<line-break>一気に上がり、奇跡の名将と呼ばれたが、<line-break>ロストールの被害も甚大なものがあった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared after this battle, and he was hailed as a miracle-working commander. Yet Rostorl also suffered immense losses.<end>"
KO: "이 싸움으로 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불렸지만, 로스톨의 피해도 막대했다.<end>"

## 1620080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>我、ロストール王<line-break>セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>汝、ゼネテス・ファーロスを<line-break>ファーロス家当主と認め、その功により<line-break>ロストール王国軍総司令に任ずる。<end>"
EN: "By the Covenant of the Dragon's Bond, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, I, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby recognize you, Xenetes Pharos, as head of House Pharos and, in honor of your service, appoint you commander-in-chief of the army of the Kingdom of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 나, 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 그대 제네테스 파로스를 파로스 가문의 당주로 인정하고 그 공로로 로스톨 왕국군 총사령관에 임명한다.<end>"

## 1620081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、最高の殊勲は<line-break>ファーロスのドラ息子に<line-break>奪われるとはな。<end>"
EN: "Damn it. To think the Pharos family's wastrel son would claim the highest distinction.<end>"
KO: "큭, 최고의 공훈을 파로스의 망나니 아들에게 빼앗기다니.<end>"

## 1620082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "第二の殊勲、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "For the second-highest distinction: <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "제2의 공훈, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1620083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、第二の殊勲も<line-break>ファーロスのゆかりの者か…！<end>"
EN: "And even the second-highest distinction goes to someone connected with House Pharos...!<end>"
KO: "게다가 두 번째 공훈도 파로스와 연이 있는 자인가…!<end>"

## 1620084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、行け。<end>"
EN: "Go on.<end>"
KO: "자, 가.<end>"

## 1620085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>我、ロストール王<line-break>セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>その功により、汝、<value:$28>に<line-break>騎士の証である盾を与え、<line-break>白竜騎士、竜字将軍に叙任する。<end>…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>ロストール王セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>女人の身である汝に、特例をもって<line-break>騎士の証である盾を与え、<line-break>白竜騎士、竜字将軍に叙任する。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0By the Covenant of the Dragon's Bond, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, I, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby grant you, <value:$28>, the shield that marks a knight and, in honor of your deeds, invest you as a White Dragon Knight and Dragon-Sigil General.<end>By the Covenant of the Dragon's Bond, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby makes a special exception for you as a woman, granting you the shield that marks a knight and investing you as a White Dragon Knight and Dragon-Sigil General.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라, 나 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 그 공적을 기려 그대 <value:$28>에게 기사임을 증명하는 방패를 내리고 백룡기사, 용자 장군에 임명한다.<end>…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 여인의 몸인 그대에게 특별히 기사임을 증명하는 방패를 내리고 백룡기사, 용자 장군에 임명한다.<end>"

## 1620086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールを支える将軍として<line-break>ゼネテスとともに活躍するがよい。<end>"
EN: "Serve well beside Xenetes as a general upon whom Rostorl may rely.<end>"
KO: "로스톨을 떠받치는 장군으로서 제네테스와 함께 활약하도록 해라.<end>"

## 1620087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやった、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>最高の殊勲こそ<line-break>ファーロスのドラ息子に奪われたが、<line-break>第二の殊勲は、お前だ。<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. The Pharos family's wastrel son may have claimed the highest distinction, but the second-highest is yours.<end>"
KO: "잘했다, <value:$28>. 최고의 공은 파로스의 망나니 아들에게 빼앗겼지만 두 번째 공은 네 것이다.<end>"

## 1620088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いにおける<line-break>リューガ家の面目は、保たれた。<line-break>　<line-break>俺はそれを喜んでいるのだ。<line-break>お前の成功を喜んでいるわけではない。<line-break>…かん違いはするな。<end>"
EN: "You have preserved House Ryuga's honor in this war. That is what pleases me. I am not pleased by your success itself. Do not misunderstand.<end>"
KO: "이번 싸움에서 류가 가문의 체면은 지켰다. 난 그걸 기뻐하는 거다. 네 성공을 기뻐하는 게 아니야. …착각하지 마.<end>"

## 1620089

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Count <value:$28> Ryuga of Noble.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작, <value:$28>·류가.<end>"

## 1620090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、行け。<end>"

KO: "자, 가.<end>"

## 1620091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見事であった、ノーブル伯。<end>"
EN: "You have done splendidly, Count of Noble.<end>"
KO: "훌륭했다, 노블 백작.<end>"

## 1620092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからもよくロストールを<line-break>支えてほしい。<line-break>期待している。<end>"
EN: "Continue to serve Rostorl well. I expect great things of you.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 로스톨을 잘 지탱해 주길 바란다. 기대하고 있다.<end>"

## 1620093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、肩がこった。<line-break>大げさな式典だったな。<line-break>　<line-break>お互い、これから<line-break>めんどくさいことになりそうだ。<line-break>それじゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Whew, I'm stiff. That ceremony was overblown, wasn't it? Looks like we're both in for plenty of trouble from here on. See you.<end>"
KO: "아이고, 어깨야. 거창한 식전이었군. 우리 둘 다 앞으로 귀찮아질 것 같네. 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 1620094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、用意をすませてから、<line-break>もう一度ここに来るがよい。<line-break>傭兵の集合場所に案内してやる。<end>"
EN: "Then finish your preparations and come back here. I'll take you to the mercenaries' rallying point.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 준비를 마친 뒤 다시 여기로 와라. 용병 집결지로 안내해 주지.<end>"

## 1620095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さま、<line-break>お捜しいたしておりました。<line-break>至急、王宮へ。<line-break>緊急のご用件で、<line-break>エリス王妃がお呼びです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have been looking for you. You must come to the palace at once. Queen Eris has summoned you on an urgent matter.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 찾고 있었습니다. 급히 왕궁으로 오십시오. 긴급한 용건으로 에리스 왕비님께서 부르십니다.<end>"

## 1620096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐに馬車の用意を。<end>"
EN: "Have a carriage prepared at once.<end>"
KO: "당장 마차를 준비해.<end>"

## 1620097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がお送りいたします。<line-break>急ぎましょう。<end>"
EN: "I shall escort you. We must hurry.<end>"
KO: "제가 모시겠습니다. 서두르시죠.<end>"

## 1620098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれたな、ノーブル伯。<line-break>火急の用件ゆえ、手短に話そう。<line-break>　<line-break>近く、ディンガルがロストール侵攻を<line-break>もくろんでいることがわかった。<line-break>ロセンの次はこの国というわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for coming, Count of Noble. The matter is urgent, so I shall be brief. We have learned that Dyneskal is planning to invade Rostorl soon. After Rocen, this country is next.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 줬다, 노블 백작. 급한 일이니 짧게 말하지. 조만간 딘갈이 로스톨 침공을 노리고 있다는 사실을 알아냈다. 로센 다음은 이 나라라는 거지.<end>"

## 1620099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が軍は、ノヴィン・ファーロスを<line-break>総司令とし、迎撃軍を出す。<line-break>そなたも参戦してほしい。<line-break>配属は、<line-break>ゼネテス・ファーロス副司令の<line-break>副官だ。<end>"
EN: "We shall dispatch an army to meet the invasion, with Novin Pharos as commander-in-chief. I want you to join the campaign. You will serve as adjutant to Deputy Commander Xenetes Pharos.<end>"
KO: "우리 군은 노빈 파로스를 총사령으로 삼아 요격군을 출진시킨다. 그대도 참전해 주었으면 한다. 배속은 제네테스 파로스 부사령의 부관이다.<end>"

## 1620100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞けばそなたは冒険者として、<line-break>活躍しているとのこと。<line-break>ゼネテスとは、気も合おう。<end>"
EN: "I hear you have distinguished yourself as an adventurer. I expect you and Xenetes will get along well.<end>"
KO: "들으니 너는 모험자로 활약하고 있다더군. 제네테스와도 마음이 잘 맞겠지.<end>"

## 1620101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、知らなかったのか？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの者は、我が軍の総司令<line-break>我が兄ノヴィン・ファーロスの息子。<line-break>私の甥なのだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You did not know? He is the son of my brother, Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of our army. Xenetes is my nephew.<end>"
KO: "호오, 몰랐나? 저자는 우리 군 총사령관이자 내 형인 노빈 파로스의 아들. 내 조카다.<end>"

## 1620102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。あれでも、貴族なのだ。<line-break>信じられないだろうが。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Believe it or not, even he is a noble.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 저래 보여도 귀족이다. 믿기 어렵겠지만.<end>"

## 1620103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたの兄レムオン卿には、<line-break>王都守備軍を率いてもらう。<line-break>　<line-break>離ればなれでの戦になるが、<line-break>兄なしでも、そなたは、<line-break>立派に戦えよう？<end>"
EN: "Your brother, Lord Lemghon, will command the force defending the capital. You will fight apart, but surely you can acquit yourself well without your brother at your side?<end>"
KO: "그대의 형 레무온 경에게는 왕도 수비군을 맡길 것이다. 떨어져 싸우게 되지만 형이 없어도 그대는 훌륭히 싸울 수 있겠지?<end>"

## 1620104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼する！<end>"
EN: "Pardon the intrusion!<end>"
KO: "이만 실례하겠다!<end>"

## 1620105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや、レムオン卿。<line-break>突然、何ごとか？<end>"
EN: "Well, well, Lord Lemghon. What brings you here so suddenly?<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 레무온 경. 갑자기 무슨 일인가?<end>"

## 1620106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が陣営に<line-break>ノーブル伯の名がなかったので、<line-break>不審に思いまして。<line-break>しかも、エリス王妃<line-break>じきじきのお呼びがあったと<line-break>聞きましたから…。<end>"
EN: "I found it suspicious that the Count of Noble's name was absent from my forces. Then I heard that Queen Eris had personally issued a summons...<end>"
KO: "우리 진영에 노블 백작의 이름이 없어서 이상하게 생각했습니다. 게다가 에리스 왕비께서 직접 부르셨다고 들었으니까요….<end>"

## 1620107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0不肖の我が弟のこと。<line-break>気になりましたので、<line-break>お伺いにあがったのです。<end>不肖の我が妹のこと。<line-break>気になりましたので、<line-break>お伺いにあがったのです。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0I was concerned about my unworthy younger brother, so I came to inquire.<end>I was concerned about my unworthy younger sister, so I came to inquire.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0못난 제 남동생 일이 신경 쓰여 찾아왔습니다.<end>못난 제 여동생 일이 신경 쓰여 찾아왔습니다.<end>"

## 1620108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ふふふ。<line-break>弟思いだな、レムオン卿は。<end>ふふふ。<line-break>妹思いだな、レムオン卿は。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Heh. You do dote on your younger brother, Lord Lemghon.<end>Heh. You do dote on your younger sister, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0후후후. 남동생을 끔찍이 아끼는군, 레무온 경은.<end>후후후. 여동생을 끔찍이 아끼는군, 레무온 경은.<end>"

## 1620109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯には、<line-break>ゼネテス卿の副官をやってもらう。<end>"
EN: "The Count of Noble will serve as Lord Xenetes's adjutant.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작에게는 제네테스 경의 부관을 맡기겠다.<end>"

## 1620110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの男の！？<line-break>理由をご説明願いたい。<end>"
EN: "His adjutant!? I demand an explanation.<end>"
KO: "그 남자의!? 이유를 설명해 주시오.<end>"

## 1620111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス卿の部隊は、<line-break>傭兵を使っている。<line-break>貴族では扱いづらい部隊だ。　　<line-break>だが、冒険者でもある<line-break>ノーブル伯ならば、傭兵たちも、<line-break>たやすく受け入れよう？<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes's unit employs mercenaries, who are difficult for a noble to command. But the Count of Noble is also an adventurer. Surely the mercenaries will readily accept such an officer?<end>"
KO: "제네테스 경의 부대는 용병을 쓰고 있다. 귀족이 다루기엔 까다로운 부대지. 하지만 모험가이기도 한 노블 백작이라면 용병들도 쉽게 받아들이겠지?<end>"

## 1620112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>くだらん理屈を！<end>"
EN: "Ghh... What a ridiculous argument!<end>"
KO: "큭…. 시시한 논리를!<end>"

## 1620113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国の有事である。<line-break>人材は、有効に使わねばな。<end>"
EN: "The nation is in peril. We must make effective use of every capable person.<end>"
KO: "나라의 비상사태다. 인재는 유용하게 써야지.<end>"

## 1620114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、ノーブル伯、<line-break>すぐにゼネテス卿のもとへ。<end>"
EN: "Now, Count of Noble, report to Lord Xenetes at once.<end>"
KO: "그럼 노블 백작, 바로 제네테스 경에게 가시오.<end>"

## 1620115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…行け。<line-break>仕方あるまい。<line-break>功のひとつでも立ててこい！<end>"
EN: "Go. There is no other choice. At least come back with some distinction to your name!<end>"
KO: "…가라. 어쩔 수 없군. 공이라도 하나 세우고 와라!<end>"

## 1620116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>殿を<line-break>お連れしました、ゼネテス様。<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes, I have brought <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님을 모셔 왔습니다, 제네테스 님.<end>"

## 1620117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "入ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Come in.<end>"
KO: "들어와.<end>"

## 1620118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれたな、ノーブル伯。<line-break>歓迎するぜ。<end>"
EN: "Glad you came, Count of Noble. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, 노블 백작. 환영한다.<end>"

## 1620119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今まで身分を明かさなかったのは、<line-break>あやまるよ。わりぃ、わりぃ。<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、俺も自分が貴族だってこと<line-break>忘れてたんでね。<end>"
EN: "Sorry I never told you about my station. Sorry, sorry. Though to be fair, I'd forgotten I was a noble myself.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 신분을 밝히지 않은 건 미안해. 미안, 미안. 다만 나도 내가 귀족이라는 걸 잊고 있었거든.<end>"

## 1620120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス王妃に言われて、<line-break>来たんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Queen Eris sent you, right?<end>"
KO: "에리스 왕비가 보내서 온 거지?<end>"

## 1620121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この部隊に配属された本当の理由、<line-break>聞いておく気はあるか？<end>"
EN: "Want to know the real reason you were assigned to this unit?<end>"
KO: "이 부대에 배속된 진짜 이유, 들어 둘 생각은 있나?<end>"

## 1620122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞く<end>"
EN: "Ask why<end>"
KO: "듣는다<end>"

## 1620123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞かない<end>"
EN: "Don't ask<end>"
KO: "안 듣는다<end>"

## 1620124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人質だ。<end>"
EN: "You're a hostage.<end>"
KO: "인질이다.<end>"

## 1620125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "守備軍として<line-break>王都に残るレムオンが、<line-break>謀反を起こさないためのな。<end>"
EN: "Insurance that Lemghon won't rebel while he remains in the capital with its defense force.<end>"
KO: "수비군으로 왕도에 남는 레무온이 모반을 일으키지 못하게 하려는 거지.<end>"

## 1620126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仮に、お前さんを見捨てて<line-break>謀反を起こしても、<line-break>　<line-break>血族を見捨てた奴になんざ、<line-break>誰もつかねぇからな。<line-break>レムオンもあとが続かない。<end>"
EN: "Even if he abandoned you and rebelled anyway, no one would follow a man who'd cast aside his own kin. Lemghon's rebellion would go no further.<end>"
KO: "설령 널 버리고 반란을 일으켜도 혈족을 버린 놈에게는 아무도 붙지 않아. 레무온도 뒤가 이어지지 않을 거다.<end>"

## 1620127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴もえげつない方法で、<line-break>レムオンに圧力をかけるよな、<line-break>まったく。<end>"
EN: "My aunt certainly found a vicious way to put pressure on Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "숙모도 참 지독한 방법으로 레무온을 압박하는군, 정말.<end>"

## 1620128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あそこまで仲がわりぃと<line-break>手のつけようがないね。<end>"
EN: "When two people hate each other that much, there's no helping them.<end>"
KO: "저 정도로 사이가 나쁘면 손쓸 방법이 없네.<end>"

## 1620129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、レムオンが本当に<line-break>謀反を起こす気があるかどうかは、<line-break>それほど問題じゃない。<line-break>要は、警告なのさ。<line-break>隙は見せないっていうな。<line-break>政治的な戦いってやつさ。<end>"
EN: "Whether Lemghon truly intends to rebel isn't all that important. The point is to send a warning: we won't show any weakness. It's a political battle.<end>"
KO: "아니, 레무온이 정말 반란을 일으킬 생각인지 아닌지는 그리 중요하지 않아. 요점은 경고야. 빈틈을 보이지 않겠다는 거지. 정치적인 싸움이란 거야.<end>"

## 1620130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、お前さんも、少しは兄貴の立場、<line-break>覚えておいてくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>ことが面倒にならないように、<line-break>見といてくれよな。<end>"
EN: "Just keep your brother's position in mind. Try to make sure this doesn't turn into an even bigger mess.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 자네도 형의 입장을 조금은 기억해 줘. 일이 귀찮아지지 않도록 지켜봐 줘.<end>"

## 1620131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>ま、聞きたくないもんは、<line-break>しゃーないか。<end>"
EN: "All right. If you don't want to hear it, I suppose that's that.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 뭐, 듣고 싶지 않은 건 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 1620132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、この戦いについて、<line-break>詳しいことを話しておこうか。<line-break>　<line-break>はじめに言っておくが、<line-break>この戦い、このままいけば、<line-break>確実に負ける。<end>"
EN: "Then let me tell you about the war. I'll say this up front: if things keep going as they are, we're certain to lose.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이 전투에 대해 자세히 이야기해 두지. 먼저 말해 두는데, 이 전투는 이대로 가면 확실히 진다.<end>"

## 1620133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、待て。ちゃんと説明するって。<line-break>長くなるが、聞くか？<end>"
EN: "Hold on, I'll explain. It'll take a while. Want to hear it?<end>"
KO: "잠깐만. 제대로 설명할 테니까. 길어질 텐데 들을래?<end>"

## 1620134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞く<end>"
EN: "Listen<end>"
KO: "듣는다<end>"

## 1620135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞かない<end>"
EN: "Don't listen<end>"
KO: "안 듣는다<end>"

## 1620136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まず、密偵が持ってきた情報だが、<line-break>ディンガルの首都エンシャントには、<line-break>大量の物資が運び込まれているそうだ。<line-break>それに合わせて、<line-break>大兵力も集結している。<end>"
EN: "First, here's what our spies reported. Vast quantities of supplies are being brought into Ancient, Dyneskal's capital, and a large army is gathering there as well.<end>"
KO: "우선 밀정이 가져온 정보인데, 딘갈 수도 엔샨트에는 대량의 물자가 운반되고 있대. 거기에 맞춰 대병력도 집결하고 있고.<end>"

## 1620137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これだけの兵と物資を抱えて、<line-break>ディンガルとロストールを隔てている<line-break>分断の山脈を越えることはできない。<line-break>おそらく海路で来るはず…。<line-break>ロストール軍はそう判断して、<line-break>上陸時を叩く作戦に出た。<end>"
EN: "They can't cross the Dividing Mountains between Dyneskal and Rostorl while burdened with that many soldiers and supplies. They'll most likely come by sea. That's the Rostorl army's conclusion, so the plan is to strike as they land.<end>"
KO: "이만한 병력과 물자를 안고 딘갈과 로스톨을 가르는 분단의 산맥을 넘을 수는 없다. 아마 해로로 올 것이다…. 로스톨군은 그렇게 판단해 상륙할 때를 노려 치는 작전에 나섰다.<end>"

## 1620138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どっちも正攻法だ。<line-break>そう間違っちゃいない。<line-break>だが、敵の将軍はアンギルダンだ。<line-break>集めている兵と物資は、陽動。<line-break>おそらく、海路と思わせて、<line-break>山越えをするつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "Both sides are following the conventional approach. It's not exactly wrong. But the enemy general is Angeerdan. The troops and supplies he's gathering are a diversion. My guess is that he'll make us expect an attack by sea, then cross the mountains.<end>"
KO: "둘 다 정공법이야. 크게 틀리진 않았지. 하지만 적 장군은 앙길단이다. 모으고 있는 병사와 물자는 양동. 아마 해로라고 믿게 하고 산을 넘을 생각일 거야.<end>"

## 1620139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "海の方へロストール軍をおびき寄せ、<line-break>自らは精鋭を率い、山を越え、<line-break>街道筋を制圧して、前線を築く。<line-break>その後、現地で物資調達し、<line-break>急場の備えを整えてのち、<line-break>ゆっくりと兵と物資を運ぶ。<end>"
EN: "He'll lure the Rostorl army toward the coast while leading an elite force over the mountains himself. He'll seize the roads and establish a front, then requisition supplies locally, make whatever immediate preparations are needed, and bring the rest of his troops and supplies across at his leisure.<end>"
KO: "해안 쪽으로 로스톨군을 유인하고, 자신은 정예를 이끌고 산을 넘어 가도를 제압해 전선을 구축한다. 그 뒤 현지에서 물자를 조달해 급한 준비를 마친 뒤 천천히 병력과 물자를 운반한다.<end>"

## 1620140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつをやられたら厳しい。<line-break>だが、この意見を軍議にかけたら、<line-break>あっさりけられた。<line-break>まあ、無茶な奇襲作戦だからな。<line-break>あり得ないと言われたら、<line-break>それまでなんだがね。<end>"
EN: "If he pulls that off, we'll be in trouble. But when I presented this theory to the war council, they dismissed it out of hand. It is a reckless surprise attack, after all. If they say it's impossible, there's not much more I can argue.<end>"
KO: "이걸 당하면 곤란해. 하지만 이 의견을 군의에 올렸더니 단번에 기각됐어. 뭐, 무모한 기습 작전이긴 하니까. 말도 안 된다고 하면 할 말은 없지만.<end>"

## 1620141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、それもいい。<line-break>聞いたところでやることは一緒だ。<line-break>肝すわってるな。さすがだ。<line-break>ただ、この戦い、<line-break>覚悟はしておいてくれ。<line-break>いやーな予感が、してるんでな。<end>"
EN: "Fair enough. Hearing it wouldn't change what you have to do. You've got nerve―I expected no less. Still, brace yourself for this battle. I've got a bad feeling about it.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 그것도 좋지. 들어도 할 일은 같아. 배짱 있네. 역시 대단해. 다만 이번 싸움은 각오해 둬. 안 좋은 예감이 들어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1620142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の予測が外れてくれると<line-break>いいんだが…。<end>"
EN: "I hope my prediction is wrong...<end>"
KO: "내 예상이 빗나가 주면 좋겠는데….<end>"

## 1620143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いざってときには<line-break>頼りにしてる。<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "When things get bad, I'm counting on you. I'll be relying on you.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 위급할 때는 믿고 의지할 테니 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1620144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いざってときには、<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<line-break>お互い、死にたくねぇもんな。<end>"
EN: "When things get bad, I'm counting on you. Neither of us wants to die, after all.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 만일의 경우엔 잘 부탁한다. 서로 죽고 싶진 않잖아.<end>"

## 1620145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネテスの予測は的中した。<end>"
EN: "But Xenetes' prediction proved correct.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스의 예상은 적중했다.<end>"

## 1620146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯<value:$28>ですね。<end>"
EN: "You must be <value:$28>, Count of Noble.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작 <value:$28>이군요.<end>"

## 1620147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるべくお早く<line-break>レムオン卿のお屋敷に　　　　　　　<line-break>お戻りください。<line-break>ディンガル帝国に動きがありまして、<line-break>近く出兵があるやもしれませぬ。<end>"
EN: "Please return to Lord Lemghon's estate as soon as you can. The Dyneskal Empire has been making moves, and there may soon be a military deployment.<end>"
KO: "가능한 한 빨리 레무온 경의 저택으로 돌아가 주십시오. 딘갈 제국에 움직임이 있어 곧 출병이 있을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1620148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その際には、ノーブル伯にも、<line-break>お呼びがかかります。<line-break>確か、冒険者をやっていますね。<end>"
EN: "If that happens, you too will be called upon as Count of Noble. You're working as an adventurer, if I recall.<end>"
KO: "그때는 노블 백작도 소집될 겁니다. 분명 모험자를 하고 계셨죠.<end>"

## 1620149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それならば、今引き受けている仕事に<line-break>ケリをつけておくことをお忘れなく。<line-break>　<line-break>貴族の道楽で冒険者をやっていると<line-break>思われたくはないでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Then don't forget to finish whatever work you've already taken on. You wouldn't want anyone thinking adventuring is merely an aristocrat's pastime for you, would you?<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 지금 맡고 있는 일을 마무리하는 걸 잊지 마십시오. 귀족의 취미로 모험자를 한다고 생각되고 싶진 않겠죠?<end>"

## 1620150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、確かにお伝えいたしました。<line-break>くれぐれも、なるべく<line-break>お早くお戻りください。<end>"
EN: "There. I have delivered the message. Please make every effort to return as soon as possible.<end>"
KO: "그럼 분명히 전해 드렸습니다. 부디 가능한 한 빨리 돌아와 주십시오.<end>"

## 1620151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>ロストール王セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>女人の身である汝に、特例をもって<line-break>騎士の証である盾を与え、<line-break>白竜騎士、竜字将軍に叙任する。<end>"
EN: "…By the Dragoncord Covenant graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl… hereby makes an exception for you, though you are a woman, and bestows upon you the shield that marks a knight, appointing you White Dragon Knight and Dragon-Sigil General.<end>"
KO: "…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 여자의 몸인 그대에게 특별히 기사의 증표인 방패를 내리고 백룡기사, 용자장군으로 임명한다.<end>"

## 1620152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不肖の我が妹のこと。<line-break>気になりましたので、<line-break>お伺いにあがったのです。<end>"
EN: "My less-than-worthy sister has been on my mind, so I came to inquire after her.<end>"
KO: "변변치 못한 제 여동생 일입니다. 걱정되어 찾아왔습니다.<end>"

## 1620153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break>妹思いだな、レムオン卿は。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You do care for your sister, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 여동생을 아끼는군, 레무온 경은.<end>"

## 1620154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec162-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>竜字将軍の称号を得た。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> received the title of Dragon-Sigil General.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 용자 장군의 칭호를 얻었다.<end>"

## 1630001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍から承っております。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "We've received word from the Vermilion Bird General. Please go on through!<end>"
KO: "주작장군께 말씀 들었습니다. 들어가십시오!<end>"

## 1630002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれた、　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad you came, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 주었구나, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1630003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いよいよ、ロストールへ進攻する。<line-break>ついては、お主にわしの副将になって<line-break>傭兵部隊を率いてほしい。<end>"
EN: "At last, we march on Rostorl. I want you to serve as my second-in-command and lead the mercenary forces.<end>"
KO: "마침내 로스톨로 진공한다. 이에 너에게 내 부장이 되어 용병부대를 이끌어 주길 바란다.<end>"

## 1630004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主のことなら<line-break>冒険者時代によく知っておる。<line-break>お主がおれば百人力なのじゃが…。<end>"
EN: "I know you well from your adventuring days. With you at my side, we'd have the strength of a hundred men...<end>"
KO: "네 일이라면 모험자 시절부터 잘 알고 있다. 네가 있으면 백 명 몫인데….<end>"

## 1630005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1630006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1630007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、お主さえ来れば<line-break>他に待つものはない。<line-break>さっそく、出撃じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Good. Now that you're here, there's no one else to wait for. We march at once!<end>"
KO: "그런가, 네가 왔으니 더 기다릴 사람은 없다. 당장 출격이다!<end>"

## 1630008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアの世界統一構想のもと<line-break>南方攻略軍司令、朱雀将軍アンギルダンが<line-break>ついにロストール攻略に動き出した。<end>"
EN: "Under Emperor Nemea's plan to unite the world, Angeerdan, the Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army, at last launched the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 세계 통일 구상 아래 남방 공략군 사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이 마침내 로스톨 공략에 나섰다.<end>"

## 1630009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　大兵力を予想したロストール側は<line-break>　　　　　　大量輸送の必要から、<line-break>　　　　　帝国軍が海路を採ると読み、<line-break>　　　　その上陸時を叩く作戦にでた。<end>"
EN: "Expecting a massive force that would require transport on a vast scale, Rostorl concluded that the Imperial Army would come by sea and planned to strike as it landed.<end>"
KO: " 대병력을 예상한 로스톨 측은 대량 수송이 필요하다는 점에서, 제국군이 해로를 택할 것으로 보고, 그 상륙 시점을 치는 작전을 세웠다.<end>"

## 1630010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　しかし、アンギルダンは<line-break>　　少数の精兵を率いて分断の山脈を越え、<line-break>　　　　　　大街道沿いの都市を<line-break>　　　　　電撃的に次々と占領した。　<end>"
EN: "But Angeerdan led a small force of elite troops across the Dividing Mountains, then seized the cities along the main highway one after another in a lightning advance.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단은 소수 정예병을 이끌고 분단의 산맥을 넘어 대가도 연변의 도시들을 전격적으로 차례차례 점령했다.<end>"

## 1630011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは<line-break>　　　　　　歴史上初の壮挙である。<line-break>　　　　　こうしてディンガル軍は<line-break>　　　　王都ロストールに王手をかけた。<line-break>　　　事ここに及び、ロストール軍総司令<line-break>　　　　ノヴィンは急きょ、軍を反転、<line-break>　　　アンギルダン率いるディンガル軍を<line-break>　　　　　迎え撃つべく、急行した。<end>"
EN: "It was the first time in history that an army had crossed the Dividing Mountains, a remarkable feat. The Dyneskal Army now had the royal capital, Rostorl, in check. Faced with this turn of events, Novin, commander-in-chief of Rostorl's forces, hastily reversed course and rushed to intercept the Dyneskal Army led by Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "분단의 산맥을 군대가 넘은 것은 역사상 최초의 위업이었다. 이렇게 딩갈군은 왕도 로스톨을 눈앞에 두게 되었다. 사태가 이에 이르자 로스톨군 총사령 노빈은 급히 군을 돌려 앙길단이 이끄는 딩갈군을 맞아 싸우기 위해 달려갔다.<end>"

## 1630012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　事ここに及び、ロストール軍総司令<line-break>　　　　ノヴィンは急きょ、軍を反転、<line-break>　　　アンギルダン率いるディンガル軍を<line-break>　　　　　迎え撃つべく、急行した。<end>"
EN: "Faced with this turn of events, Novin, commander-in-chief of Rostorl's forces, hastily reversed course and rushed to intercept the Dyneskal Army led by Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "사태가 이 지경에 이르자 로스톨군 총사령관 노빈은 급히 군을 돌려 앙길단이 이끄는 딘갈군을 맞아 싸우기 위해 급행했다.<end>"

## 1630013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは<line-break>　歴史上初の壮挙である。こうして、<line-break>帝国軍は王都ロストールに王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "It was the first time in history that an army had crossed the Dividing Mountains, a remarkable feat. The Imperial Army now had the royal capital, Rostorl, in check.<end>"
KO: "분단의 산맥을 군대가 넘은 것은 역사상 처음인 위업이었다. 그렇게 제국군은 왕도 로스톨을 코앞에 두었다.<end>"

## 1630014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうにもロストールの将軍は無能よの。<line-break>これでは、その下で戦う<line-break>ロストールの兵がかわいそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl's general is hopelessly incompetent. I pity the Rostorl soldiers who have to fight under such a commander.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 로스톨의 장군은 무능하군. 저런 자 밑에서 싸우는 로스톨 병사들이 불쌍할 지경이야.<end>"

## 1630015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍はわしに戦いを挑むべく<line-break>隊列がバラバラになるのもかまわずに<line-break>夜を日に継いで急行軍で来とるという。<end>"
EN: "I hear the Rostorl army is racing here day and night on a forced march to challenge me, heedless of its formation falling into disarray.<end>"
KO: "로스톨군은 내게 싸움을 걸기 위해 대열이 흐트러지는 것도 아랑곳하지 않고 밤낮없이 급행군으로 오고 있다더군.<end>"

## 1630016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むざむざ、やられに来るようなものだ。<line-break>最初の一手を間違えたからには、<line-break>もう、ロストールには勝機はない。<end>"
EN: "They may as well be marching straight to their deaths. Having blundered with their opening move, Rostorl no longer has any chance of victory.<end>"
KO: "제 발로 당하러 오는 거나 마찬가지야. 첫 수를 잘못 둔 이상 이제 로스톨에는 승산이 없어.<end>"

## 1630017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら連中は、地獄へ送って<line-break>頭も身体も冷たくしてやらねば、<line-break>冷静にものを考えられぬと見た。<end>"
EN: "It seems they won't be able to think clearly until we've sent them to hell and cooled their heads―and their bodies.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 놈들은 지옥으로 보내 머리와 몸을 차갑게 식혀 주지 않으면 냉정하게 생각할 수 없는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1630018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皆の者！　出撃だ！<line-break>さんざんに破り、ディンガル帝国の名を<line-break>恐怖とともに奴らの脳髄にたたき込め！<end>"
EN: "All of you, move out! Crush them utterly, and hammer the name of the Dyneskal Empire, together with the terror it inspires, into their very brains!<end>"
KO: "전원 출격이다! 철저히 짓밟고 딘갈 제국의 이름을 공포와 함께 놈들의 뇌리에 새겨 넣어라!<end>"

## 1630019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現在、我が軍の主力が、敵の先鋒と交戦中です。<line-break>我々は、この隙に兵が集結しきっていない、<line-break>本陣を急襲します。<line-break>アンギルダン将軍も別経路で本陣をつきます。<line-break>この作戦の成否が、勝敗を分けましょう。<end>"
EN: "Our main force is currently engaging the enemy vanguard. We will use this opening to launch a surprise attack on their headquarters before their troops have fully assembled. General Angeerdan will strike the headquarters by another route. The outcome of the battle will hinge on this operation.<end>"
KO: "현재 아군 주력이 적의 선봉과 교전 중입니다. 우리는 이 틈에 병력이 아직 다 모이지 않은 본진을 급습합니다. 앙길단 장군도 다른 경로로 본진을 찌를 것입니다. 이 작전의 성패가 승패를 가를 겁니다.<end>"

## 1630020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、傭兵部隊、出撃します！<end>"
EN: "Mercenary unit, move out!<end>"
KO: "그럼 용병 부대, 출격합니다!<end>"

## 1630021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む！　傭兵か！？<line-break>ここから先には行かせんぞ！<end>"
EN: "What?! A mercenary?! You're not getting past me!<end>"
KO: "뭐! 용병인가!? 이 앞은 지나가게 둘 수 없다!<end>"

## 1630022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新手か！<line-break>これ以上は、突破させん！<end>"
EN: "More of them! You won't get past me!<end>"
KO: "새로운 적인가! 더는 돌파하게 두지 않겠다!<end>"

## 1630023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こいつは手強いです。<end>"
EN: "T-this is a tough one.<end>"
KO: "이, 이 녀석은 만만치 않습니다.<end>"

## 1630024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何人来ようと同じだ。<line-break>侵略者め、覚悟！！<end>"
EN: "No matter how many of you come, it makes no difference. Invaders, prepare yourselves!<end>"
KO: "몇 명이 오든 마찬가지다. 침략자 놈들, 각오해라!!<end>"

## 1630025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすが副将！<line-break>さあ、急ぎましょう！<line-break>この先が、本隊との合流地点です。<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from the deputy commander! Now, we must hurry! The rendezvous point with the main force is just ahead.<end>"
KO: "역시 부장! 자, 서두릅시다! 이 앞이 본대와의 합류 지점입니다.<end>"

## 1630026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これは！<line-break>本隊はいったい！？<end>"
EN: "W-what!? What happened to the main force!?<end>"
KO: "이, 이건! 본대는 대체!?<end>"

## 1630027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おのれ、ディンガル！<line-break>もうここまで来るとは！！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, Dyneskal! How did you get this far already!?<end>"
KO: "이놈, 딘갈! 벌써 여기까지 오다니!!<end>"

## 1630028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早いな、<value:$28>。<line-break>わしより先にここにたどり着くとは。<line-break>さすがじゃ。<end>"
EN: "You're quick, <value:$28>. To think you'd make it here before me. Impressive.<end>"
KO: "빠르군, <value:$28>. 나보다 먼저 여기까지 오다니. 역시 대단해.<end>"

## 1630029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他の敵は、お主が戦っておる間に<line-break>わしが倒しておいた。<line-break>この戦い、大勝じゃわ。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with the other enemies while you were fighting. This battle is a resounding victory.<end>"
KO: "다른 적은 네가 싸우는 동안 내가 쓰러뜨렸다. 이번 싸움은 대승이다.<end>"

## 1630030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍！　我が軍の主力部隊は、<line-break>敵先鋒軍の撃破に成功したそうです！<end>"
EN: "General! Word has come that our main force has defeated the enemy vanguard!<end>"
KO: "장군! 아군 주력 부대가 적 선봉군 격파에 성공했다고 합니다!<end>"

## 1630031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、完璧じゃな。<line-break>すぐに次の手を打つとしようか。<line-break>いったん本陣に戻るぞ！<end>"
EN: "Good. Everything has gone perfectly. Let us make our next move at once. Back to headquarters!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 완벽하군. 곧바로 다음 수를 쓰자. 일단 본진으로 돌아간다!<end>"

## 1630032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、来たか<value:$28>。<line-break>さすがじゃ。<line-break>これでこの戦いの勝ちは決まったわ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, there you are, <value:$28>. Impressive as ever. Our victory is assured now.<end>"
KO: "오, 왔구나 <value:$28>. 역시 대단하군. 이걸로 이 싸움은 이긴 것이나 다름없다.<end>"

## 1630033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、よくやった。<line-break>お主が最高殊勲者じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. You have earned the highest distinction!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘했다. 네가 최고 공로자다!<end>"

## 1630034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この功をもって、わしは<line-break>お主をディンガルの騎士に推薦し、<line-break>ここで仮叙任を行おうと思う。<end>"
EN: "For this achievement, I intend to recommend you for knighthood in Dyneskal and grant you a provisional investiture here and now.<end>"
KO: "이 공로로 나는 자네를 딩갈의 기사로 추천하고 여기서 임시 임명을 하려 한다.<end>"

## 1630035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひざまずくがよい。<end>"
EN: "Kneel.<end>"
KO: "무릎을 꿇어라.<end>"

## 1630036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我、朱雀将軍アンギルダンは<line-break>皇帝ネメア・ランガスターの　　　　<line-break>代理として<line-break>戦場のことゆえ略式ではあるが、<line-break>汝、<value:$28>を<line-break>ディンガル帝国黒鎧騎士に叙任する。<end>"
EN: "I, Angeerdan, Vermilion Bird General, acting on behalf of Emperor Nemea Lancaster, hereby invest you, <value:$28>, as a Black-Armored Knight of the Dyneskal Empire. As we stand upon a battlefield, the ceremony must be brief.<end>"
KO: "나 주작장군 앙길단은 황제 네메아 랑가스터를 대신하여, 전장인 까닭에 약식이지만 그대 <value:$28>를 딩갈 제국 흑개 기사에 임명한다.<end>"

## 1630037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキル<line-break>グロリアシルドを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Acquired the skill Gloria Shield.<end>"
KO: "스킬 글로리아 실드를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1630038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、これは恩賞じゃ。<line-break>さらに、励んでくれ。<end>"
EN: "And here is your reward. Keep up the good work.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이건 포상이다. 더욱 힘써 주게.<end>"

## 1630039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>まずまずの戦功じゃな。<line-break>　<line-break>これは恩賞じゃ。<line-break>さらに励んでくれ。<line-break>期待しておるぞ。<end>"
EN: "A respectable achievement, <value:$28>. Here is your reward. Keep up the good work. I expect much from you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 제법 전공을 세웠구나. 이건 포상이다. 더욱 힘써라. 기대하고 있다.<end>"

## 1630040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ロストールの本軍は叩いたが<line-break>まだ王都守備軍が残っておる。<end>"
EN: "Now, we have crushed Rostorl's main army, but the force defending the royal capital remains.<end>"
KO: "자, 로스톨의 본군은 격파했지만 아직 왕도 수비군이 남아 있다.<end>"

## 1630041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恐れるほどの敵ではないが、<line-break>山越えを強行した我が軍には、<line-break>物資が不足しておる。<end>"
EN: "They are nothing to fear, but after forcing a march across the mountains, our army is short on supplies.<end>"
KO: "두려워할 정도의 적은 아니지만 산을 강행군한 우리 군은 물자가 부족하다.<end>"

## 1630042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らが動く前に<line-break>こちらはあたりから物資を徴発して<line-break>備えを完全にしておかねばならん。<end>"
EN: "Before they make a move, we must requisition supplies from the surrounding area and complete our preparations.<end>"
KO: "놈들이 움직이기 전에 우리는 주변에서 물자를 징발해 대비를 완전히 갖춰 둬야 한다.<end>"

## 1630043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸いロストール軍には、<line-break>壊滅状態の軍を<line-break>立て直せるような将はおらん。<end>"
EN: "Fortunately, Rostorl has no commander capable of rallying an army in such ruin.<end>"
KO: "다행히 로스톨군에는 괴멸 상태의 군을 다시 세울 만한 장수가 없다.<end>"

## 1630044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆえに敗走中の敵の掃討作戦は行わず、<line-break>各隊は早急に付近の村から<line-break>物資を強制徴発してもらいたい。<end>"
EN: "Therefore, we will not pursue and eliminate the fleeing enemy. Instead, every unit is to seize the supplies we need from nearby villages without delay.<end>"
KO: "따라서 도주 중인 적을 추격하는 작전은 하지 않는다. 각 부대는 서둘러 인근 마을에서 물자를 강제 징발하도록.<end>"

## 1630045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかにも略奪行為じゃ。<line-break>じゃがな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Yes, it is plunder. But listen, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "분명 약탈 행위다. 하지만 말이다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1630046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争はどのみち血塗られた道よ。<line-break>いかにとりつくろおうともな。<end>"
EN: "However we may dress it up, war is a road paved in blood.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 어차피 피로 물든 길이다. 아무리 꾸며도 마찬가지지.<end>"

## 1630047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せめて、流される血が<line-break>少なくなるように努力するまでじゃ。<line-break>責任はわしが持つ。任務の遂行を命ず。<end>"
EN: "All we can do is strive to spill as little blood as possible. I will bear the responsibility. I order you to carry out the mission.<end>"
KO: "적어도 흘릴 피를 줄이도록 노력할 뿐이다. 책임은 내가 지겠다. 임무를 수행하라.<end>"

## 1630048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様<line-break>お気持ちはお察しいたします。<line-break>ですが、命令は遂行なさらないと…。<line-break>すでに他の部隊は、<line-break>物資調達に向かっていますよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I understand how you feel. But the order must be carried out... The other units have already left to procure supplies.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 마음은 이해합니다. 하지만 명령은 수행하셔야 합니다…. 이미 다른 부대는 물자 조달에 나섰습니다.<end>"

## 1630049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2これが戦争…。<line-break>騎士になれば、避けられない道…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4命令じゃしようがないかな。<line-break>でも、人の物は<line-break>取っちゃいけないって教わったし。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5けど、略奪なんてよ。<line-break>なんか納得いかねぇよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6戦争って、こういうことなんだね。<line-break>でも、すっきりしないなあ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13何を迷う理由がある。<line-break>これが戦争だとわかってるのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10いくら命令でも…。<line-break>姉ちゃん、これじゃあ俺たち、<line-break>ボルボラと一緒じゃないかな？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14将軍の言うことは、間違っておらんぞ。<line-break>覚悟を決めい、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15将軍の言うことはわかるけど、<line-break>でも…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17略奪なんて、やばん！　やばんだわ！！<line-break>ま～ったく、下等生物はこれだから。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>18拙者、将軍の言は正しいと思いますぞ。<line-break>戦は、早く終わらせるべきです。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>19食べ物を取ってきちゃうなんて…。<line-break>でも、そうしないと<line-break>兵隊さんが死んじゃう…。<line-break>つらいところだよね、<line-break><value:$28>…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20戦いとは、こういうものだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23これが戦争というものだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27これが戦争というものだと<line-break>わかってはいても…<line-break>こういう命令は気分が悪いな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24戦争とはこういうもの…。<line-break>ご理解ください、<value:$28>様。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>25しかたないよね…。<line-break>でもつらいね。<end>略奪なんてきれいじゃないなあ。<line-break>勝つにしても、きれいに勝ちたいよね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>193私、納得できません！<line-break>いくら戦争だからって、<line-break>やってはいけないことだってあります！<line-break>略奪だなんて…ひどすぎます！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191こうしていつも、<line-break>罪もないのに虐げられる人がいるのね…<line-break>　<line-break>かわいそうだわ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2So this is war... Once you become a knight, there's no escaping this path...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I guess we have to, since it's an order. But I was taught not to take what belongs to other people.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5But plundering? This just doesn't sit right with me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6So this is what war is like. Still, it doesn't feel right.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Why hesitate? Do you understand that this is war?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Even if it is an order... Sis, doesn't this make us no better than Bolbora?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14The general is not wrong. Steel yourself, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15I understand what the general is saying, but...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Plundering is barbaric! Utterly barbaric! Honestly, this is exactly what one expects from lower life-forms.<end><if><value:$01><equal>18I believe the general speaks truly. The war should be ended as quickly as possible.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Taking people's food... But if we don't, the soldiers will die... It's a hard situation, isn't it, <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>20This is the nature of battle.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23This is what war is.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27I know this is what war is... but orders like this leave a foul taste.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24This is the nature of war... Please understand, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25I suppose there's no choice... But it still hurts.<end>There's nothing clean about plundering. If we're going to win, I'd rather win cleanly.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193I can't accept this! War or no war, there are still things we must never do! Plundering... It's too horrible!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191It's always the same. Innocent people are made to suffer... Those poor people.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2이게 전쟁…. 기사가 되면 피할 수 없는 길….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4명령이니 어쩔 수 없나. 그래도 남의 물건을 가져가면 안 된다고 배웠는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5하지만 약탈이라니. 뭔가 납득이 안 돼!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6전쟁이란 이런 거구나. 그래도 마음이 개운하지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13망설일 이유가 어디 있지. 이게 전쟁이라는 걸 알고 있는 거냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10아무리 명령이라도…. 누나, 이래선 우리도 볼보라랑 똑같은 거 아닐까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14장군 말은 틀리지 않았다. 각오를 다져라, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15장군 말은 알겠지만, 그래도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17약탈이라니, 야만적이에요! 야만적이라고요!! 정말이지, 하등생물은 이래서 안 된다니까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>18소인은 장군의 말씀이 옳다고 생각하오. 전쟁은 빨리 끝내야 하오.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19먹을 걸 빼앗아 오다니…. 그래도 그러지 않으면 병사들이 죽어 버려…. 괴로운 일이네, <value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>20싸움이란 이런 것이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23이게 전쟁이라는 것이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이게 전쟁이라는 걸 알고는 있어도… 이런 명령은 기분이 나쁘군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24전쟁이란 이런 것입니다…. 이해해 주세요, <value:$28>님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25어쩔 수 없지…. 그래도 괴롭네.<end>약탈이라니 아름답지 않네. 이기더라도 깨끗하게 이기고 싶잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193저는 납득할 수 없어요! 아무리 전쟁이라도 해서는 안 되는 일이 있어요! 약탈이라니… 너무해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191이렇게 언제나 죄도 없는데 억압받는 사람이 생기는구나… 불쌍해.<end>"

## 1630050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、敵襲！<end>"
EN: "E-enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "저, 적습이다!<end>"

## 1630051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発隊が出払って、手薄になった本陣に、<line-break>敗残兵をまとめたロストール軍が、<line-break>攻め込んできました！<end>"
EN: "With the requisition parties away and headquarters left thinly defended, the Rostorl forces have rallied their routed soldiers and launched an attack!<end>"
KO: "징발대가 출동해 본진이 비어 있는 틈에 패잔병을 모은 로스톨군이 공격해 왔습니다!<end>"

## 1630052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな…！<line-break>あそこまで崩れ去った兵を<line-break>まとめあげただと？<end>"
EN: "Impossible...! They rallied troops who had been routed so completely?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 저렇게 무너진 병사들을 다시 규합했다고?<end>"

## 1630053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありえない…！<line-break>敵将は誰だ！？<end>"
EN: "It can't be...! Who is the enemy commander!?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 적장은 누구냐!?<end>"

## 1630054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスの息子ゼネテス！<line-break>奇襲を受けた中枢部隊は壊乱！<line-break>アンギルダン様の生死は不明です！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes, son of Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos! The central units were taken by surprise and thrown into chaos! Lord Angeerdan's fate is unknown!<end>"
KO: "총사령관 노빈 파로스의 아들 제네테스! 기습을 받은 중추부대가 궤멸했습니다! 앙길단 님의 생사는 불명입니다!<end>"

## 1630056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たぞ！<line-break>ディンガルの新手だ！<end>"
EN: "Here they come! Dyneskal reinforcements!<end>"
KO: "왔다! 딩갈의 새로운 놈들이다!<end>"

## 1630057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新手か！<line-break>これ以上は、突破させん！<end>"
EN: "More of them! You won't get past me!<end>"
KO: "새로운 적인가! 더는 돌파하게 두지 않겠다!<end>"

## 1630058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル兵か！<line-break>こいつも倒すぞ！<end>"
EN: "A Dyneskal soldier! Let's take this one down too!<end>"
KO: "딩갈 병사인가! 이 녀석도 쓰러뜨린다!<end>"

## 1630059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス…、<line-break>そうか、お主がおったのか！<line-break>　<line-break>お主ならば、あの敗軍を<line-break>まとめる器量がある！<line-break>くっ、ぬかったわ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... So, you were here! You have what it takes to rally that defeated army. Damn―I miscalculated!<end>"
KO: "제네테스…. 그렇군, 네놈이 있었나! 네놈이라면 그 패잔군을 수습할 역량이 있다! 큭, 방심했군!<end>"

## 1630060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がおるとわかっておれば、<line-break>敗残兵の殲滅を優先させたものを！<end>"
EN: "Had I known you were here, I would have made wiping out the remnants my first priority!<end>"
KO: "네놈이 있는 줄 알았다면 패잔병 섬멸을 우선했을 것을!<end>"

## 1630061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とっつぁん、<line-break>悪いが、命をもらう。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, old man, but I'm taking your life.<end>"
KO: "…아저씨, 미안하지만 목숨을 받겠다.<end>"

## 1630062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ロストールの兵の<line-break>命がかかってるんでね。<end>"
EN: "The lives of Rostorl's soldiers are at stake.<end>"
KO: "…로스톨 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1630063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、そうですかと<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかんな。<line-break>　<line-break>こっちも<line-break>ディンガルの兵の命が<line-break>かかっておるのでな！<end>"
EN: "And I cannot simply hand it over because you ask. The lives of Dyneskal's soldiers are at stake as well!<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇습니까 하고 순순히 내줄 수는 없지. 이쪽도 딘갈 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있으니까!<end>"

## 1630064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！　お前も<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんの側か！<line-break>ちっ、人物は人物を呼ぶんだな。<line-break>こりゃあ、ちょっと分が悪いな。<line-break>ま、いい。なんとか運命の女神を<line-break>振り向かせるのには成功したようだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! So you've sided with old man Angeerdan too! Tch. Talent does attract talent. The odds aren't looking too good for me now. No matter. It seems I've managed to win the Goddess of Fate's favor.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 너도 앙길단 영감 쪽이냐! 쳇, 인물은 인물을 부르는 법이군. 이거 조금 불리하겠어. 뭐, 됐어. 어떻게든 운명의 여신을 돌아보게 하는 데는 성공한 모양이니까.<end>"

## 1630065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あばよ、とっつぁん、<line-break>そして<value:$28>！<line-break>生きて、また会おうぜ！<end>"
EN: "See you, old man―and you too, <value:$28>! Stay alive, and we'll meet again!<end>"
KO: "잘 있어, 아저씨, 그리고 <value:$28>! 살아서 다시 만나자!<end>"

## 1630066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう…。<line-break>ここまでたどり着いてくるとは<line-break>やるな…。<end>"
EN: "Well now... You did well to make it this far.<end>"
KO: "호오…. 여기까지 도착하다니 제법이군….<end>"

## 1630067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の名は<line-break>ゼネテス・ファーロス。<line-break>ま、覚えといてくれや。<line-break>あんたのことは知ってる。<line-break><value:$28>…だよな？<line-break>やっぱ、人物は人物を呼ぶんだな。<end>"
EN: "The name's Xenetes Pharos. You may as well remember it. I already know yours. <value:$28>, right? Talent really does attract talent.<end>"
KO: "내 이름은 제네테스 파로스. 뭐, 기억해 둬. 네 이야기는 알고 있어. <value:$28>… 맞지? 역시 인물은 인물을 부르는 법이군.<end>"

## 1630068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日のところは、退くとしよう。<line-break>なんとか運命の女神を<line-break>振り向かせるのに成功したようだしな。<end>"
EN: "I'll withdraw for today. It seems I've managed to win the Goddess of Fate's favor, after all.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이만 물러나지. 어떻게든 운명의 여신을 돌아보게 하는 데 성공한 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1630069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配するな！　ノーブル伯！<line-break>叔母貴には黙っておくからな！<end>"
EN: "Don't worry, Count of Noble! I won't tell Auntie!<end>"
KO: "걱정 마라! 노블 백작! 숙모에게는 비밀로 해 둘 테니까!<end>"

## 1630070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、さすがはゼネテスよ…。<line-break>やりおる。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Just what I'd expect of Xenetes... You're good.<end>"
KO: "후후, 역시 제네테스군…. 제법이야.<end>"

## 1630071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしがもっと若ければな…。<line-break>そうすれば、お主と力を合わせて、<line-break>あの男を討ち取れていたかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "If only I were younger... Then perhaps you and I could have joined forces and brought that man down.<end>"
KO: "내가 조금만 더 젊었다면…. 그랬다면 너와 힘을 합쳐 그 남자를 쓰러뜨릴 수 있었을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1630072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、あきらめも肝要よ。<line-break>わしの負けじゃ。　<line-break>　<line-break>運命の女神のほほえみを<line-break>あの色男に持っていかれたわい！<end>"
EN: "But one must know when to concede. I have lost. That handsome devil has stolen the Goddess of Fate's smile from me!<end>"
KO: "하지만 포기도 중요하지. 내 패배다. 운명의 여신의 미소를 그 미남에게 빼앗겨 버렸구나!<end>"

## 1630073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍！　ご無事でしたか。<end>"
EN: "General! You're safe!<end>"
KO: "장군! 무사하셨습니까.<end>"

## 1630074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、よいところに来た。<line-break>全軍に伝えよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, excellent timing. Send word to the entire army.<end>"
KO: "오, 마침 잘 왔다. 전군에 전해라.<end>"

## 1630075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "撤退じゃ！　散れ！<line-break>ドワーフの王国を抜け、<line-break>生きて帰るのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Break formation! Get out of the kingdom of the Dwarves and make it home alive!<end>"
KO: "퇴각이다! 흩어져라! 드워프 왕국을 빠져나가 살아서 돌아가라!<end>"

## 1630076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！　生きて会おうぞ！<line-break>エンシャントの政庁の前で待っておる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! We'll meet again―alive! I'll be waiting in front of the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 살아서 만나자! 엔샨트 정청 앞에서 기다리고 있겠다.<end>"

## 1630077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発隊の出撃で手薄だった<line-break>本陣を突かれた帝国軍は、<line-break>敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、総崩れとなった。<end>"
EN: "With its main camp left thinly defended by the deployment of the requisitioning force, the Imperial army was thrown into a complete rout by a surprise attack from Xenetes, who had rallied the defeated remnants with astonishing speed.<end>"
KO: "징발대 출격으로 본진이 허술해진 제국군은 패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 완전히 무너졌다.<end>"

## 1630078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生き残ったディンガル兵は、　<line-break>ドワーフの王国を抜け、<line-break>ディンガル領内に撤退。<line-break>ゼネテスは、追撃しなかった。<end>"
EN: "The surviving Dyneskal soldiers withdrew through the Dwarf kingdom into Dyneskal territory. Xenetes did not pursue them.<end>"
KO: "살아남은 딘갈 병사들은 드워프 왕국을 빠져나와 딘갈 영내로 철수했다. 제네테스는 추격하지 않았다.<end>"

## 1630079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにアンギルダンの山越えに始まった<line-break>ロストール侵攻戦は、失敗に終わった。<end>"
EN: "Thus, the invasion of Rostorl that began with Angeerdan's crossing of the mountains ended in failure.<end>"
KO: "이로써 앙길단의 산악 돌파에서 시작된 로스톨 침공전은 실패로 끝났다.<end>"

## 1630080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これによりゼネテスの声望は一気に<line-break>高まり、奇跡の名将とまで呼ばれたが、<line-break>今回の戦いでロストールが受けた被害は<line-break>甚大なものであった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared as a result, and he was even hailed as a miracle-working commander. Yet the damage Rostorl suffered in this battle was immense.<end>"
KO: "이로써 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불릴 정도가 되었지만, 이번 전투로 로스톨이 입은 피해는 막대했다.<end>"

## 1630081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、<line-break>運命の女神は残酷なものよ！<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed! To abandon me after I had come this far...<end>"
KO: "후후… 운명의 여신은 잔혹하구나! 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니….<end>"

## 1630082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... To think a man like you was... the heir to Pharos... Urgh...<end>"
KO: "제네테스여…. 자네 같은 남자가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽….<end>"

## 1630083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵の略奪部隊の場所がわかりました！<end>"
EN: "We've located the enemy raiding party!<end>"
KO: "적 약탈 부대의 위치를 알아냈습니다!<end>"

## 1630084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、ここを切り上げて、<line-break>そっちを片付けに移動する。<line-break>部隊をまとめ直すぞ！<end>"
EN: "Good. We're done here. Regroup the troops, and we'll move out to deal with them!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 여기 일은 마무리하고 저쪽을 처리하러 간다. 부대를 다시 정비해!<end>"

## 1630085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes, sir!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1630086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…じゃあな、とっつぁん。<line-break>あの世で、いつか飲み明かそうぜ。<end>"
EN: "See you, old man. Someday, in the next world, we'll drink till dawn.<end>"
KO: "…잘 가라, 영감. 저세상에서 언젠가 밤새 술이나 마시자고.<end>"

## 1630087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した　<line-break>　　　ゼネテスの奇襲により、<line-break>　アンギルダンが討ち取られると、<line-break>　　帝国軍は総崩れとなった。<end>"
EN: "When Angeerdan was slain in a surprise attack by Xenetes, who had rallied the defeated soldiers with astonishing speed, the Imperial Army collapsed into a total rout.<end>"

## 1630089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにアンギルダンの山越えに始まった<line-break>ロストール侵攻戦は、失敗に終わった。<end>"
EN: "Thus, the invasion of Rostorl, which began with Angeerdan's crossing of the mountains, ended in failure.<end>"

## 1630090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1630091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。残念じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I see... That's a pity.<end>"

## 1630092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵志願者か。<line-break>案内する。ついてくるがよい。<end>"
EN: "A prospective mercenary? I'll show you the way. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "용병 지원자인가. 안내하지. 따라와라.<end>"

## 1630093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア陛下の世界統一構想に基づき、<line-break>南方攻略軍司令アンギルダン閣下に<line-break>ロストール攻略の大任が下された。<line-break>ついては、大兵力の動員のために、<line-break>傭兵を募集することになった。<line-break>君は、遠征軍に加わる気があるのか？<end>"
EN: "In accordance with His Majesty Nemea's plan to unite the world, His Excellency Angeerdan, commander of the Southern Campaign Army, has been entrusted with the vital task of carrying out the Rostorl Campaign. We are therefore recruiting mercenaries to muster the necessary forces. Are you willing to join the expeditionary force?<end>"
KO: "네메아 폐하의 세계 통일 구상에 따라 남방 공략군 사령 앙길단 각하께 로스톨 공략의 대임이 내려졌다. 이에 대병력 동원을 위해 용병을 모집하게 됐다. 자네는 원정군에 참가할 생각이 있나?<end>"

## 1630094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "加わる<end>"
EN: "Join<end>"
KO: "합류한다<end>"

## 1630095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "加わらない<end>"
EN: "Don't join<end>"
KO: "합류하지 않는다<end>"

## 1630096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それならば、さっそく出撃だ。<line-break>傭兵部隊の一員として、<line-break>本作戦に参加してもらう。<end>"
EN: "Then we march at once. You will take part in this operation as a member of the mercenary corps.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 바로 출격이다. 용병부대의 일원으로 이번 작전에 참가해 줘.<end>"

## 1630097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアの世界統一構想のもと<line-break>南方攻略軍司令、朱雀将軍アンギルダンが　<line-break>ついにロストール攻略に動き出した。<end>"
EN: "Under Emperor Nemea's plan to unite the world, Angeerdan, the Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army, at last launched the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 세계 통일 구상 아래 남방 공략군 사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이 마침내 로스톨 공략에 나섰다.<end>"

## 1630098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大兵力の動員を予想したロストール側は<line-break>帝国軍が大量輸送の必要から、海路を採ると<line-break>予想し、その上陸地点を叩く作戦にでた。<end>"
EN: "Expecting a massive force that would require transport on a vast scale, Rostorl concluded that the Imperial Army would come by sea and planned to strike at its landing site.<end>"
KO: "대병력 동원을 예상한 로스톨은 제국군이 대량 수송 때문에 해로를 택할 것으로 보고 상륙 지점을 공격하는 작전을 세웠다.<end>"

## 1630099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、アンギルダンは、少数の精兵で<line-break>分断の山脈を越え、大街道沿いの都市を<line-break>電撃的に次々と占領した。<end>"
EN: "But Angeerdan led a small force of elite troops across the Dividing Mountains and seized the cities along the main highway one after another in a lightning advance.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단은 소수 정예병으로 분단의 산맥을 넘어 대로변 도시를 전격적으로 차례차례 점령했다.<end>"

## 1630100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは歴史上<line-break>いまだかつてない壮挙である。こうして、<line-break>帝国軍は王都ロストールに王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "It was an unprecedented feat in history for an army to cross the Dividing Mountains. The Imperial Army now had the royal capital, Rostorl, in check.<end>"
KO: "분단의 산맥을 군대가 넘은 것은 역사상 전례 없는 위업이다. 이렇게 제국군은 왕도 로스톨에 장군을 걸었다.<end>"

## 1630101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスは、<line-break>これに対して、急きょ、軍を反転させ。<line-break>アンギルダンを迎え撃つべく、急行した。<end>"
EN: "In response, Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of the Rostorl Army, hastily reversed course and rushed to intercept Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령 노빈 파로스는 이에 급히 군을 되돌려 앙길단을 맞아 싸우기 위해 달려갔다.<end>"

## 1630102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍が急きょ、軍を返してきた。<line-break>おとなしくしておればよいものを<line-break>のこのこと、今さら！<end>"
EN: "The Rostorl army has abruptly turned back. They should have stayed put, but now they come blundering back!<end>"
KO: "로스톨군이 급히 군대를 돌려 왔다. 얌전히 있을 것이지, 이제 와서 어슬렁거리며 나타나다니!<end>"

## 1630103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵は急行軍で隊列もバラバラ。　　　　　<line-break>疲労しきっている。<line-break>楽勝だ！　存分に活躍せよ。<end>"
EN: "The enemy has come by forced march. Their ranks are in disarray, and they are utterly exhausted. This will be an easy victory! Go and distinguish yourselves.<end>"
KO: "적은 급행군으로 대열도 흐트러져 있고 완전히 지쳐 있다. 쉬운 싸움이다! 마음껏 활약해라.<end>"

## 1630104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "功があったものには、恩賞を弾むぞ。　　<line-break>では、出撃！！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Those who distinguish themselves will be richly rewarded. Now, move out!<end>"
KO: "공이 있는 자에게는 후한 상을 내리겠다. 그럼, 출격!!<end>"

## 1630105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現在、我が軍の主力は、敵の先鋒と交戦中だ。<line-break>我々傭兵部隊は、この隙に<line-break>兵が集結しきっていない、本陣を急襲する。<line-break>アンギルダン将軍も別経路で本陣を突く。<line-break>この戦いの勝敗をわける作戦だ。<line-break>おくれをとるなよ！！<end>"
EN: "Our main force is engaging the enemy vanguard. While they are occupied, we mercenaries will launch a surprise attack on their headquarters before their troops have fully assembled. General Angeerdan will strike the headquarters by another route. This operation will decide the battle. Don't fall behind!<end>"
KO: "현재 우리 군 주력은 적 선봉과 교전 중이다. 우리 용병부대는 그 틈을 타 병력이 아직 집결하지 않은 본진을 급습한다. 앙길단 장군도 다른 길로 본진을 찌른다. 승패를 가를 작전이다. 뒤처지지 마라!!<end>"

## 1630106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "Right. Let's move.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 가자.<end>"

## 1630107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む！　傭兵か！？<line-break>ここから先には行かせんぞ！<end>"
EN: "What?! A mercenary?! You're not getting past me!<end>"
KO: "뭐! 용병인가!? 이 앞은 지나가게 둘 수 없다!<end>"

## 1630108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新手か！<line-break>これ以上は、突破させん！<end>"
EN: "More of them! You won't get past me!<end>"
KO: "새로운 적인가! 더는 돌파하게 두지 않겠다!<end>"

## 1630109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こいつは手強い！<end>"
EN: "T-this one's tough!<end>"
KO: "이, 이놈 강하다!<end>"

## 1630110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何人来ようと、同じだ。<line-break>侵略者め、覚悟！！<end>"
EN: "No matter how many of you come, it makes no difference. Invaders, prepare yourselves!<end>"
KO: "몇 명이 오든 똑같다. 침략자 놈들, 각오해라!!<end>"

## 1630111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やるじゃないか。<line-break>このことは将軍に報告しておこう。<line-break>　<line-break>では、先を急ごう。<line-break>この先が、本隊との合流地点だ。<end>"
EN: "Not bad. I'll make sure the general hears of this. Now, let's hurry on. The rendezvous point with the main force is just ahead.<end>"
KO: "제법인데. 이건 장군께 보고해 두지. 그럼 서두르자. 이 앞이 본대와 합류할 지점이다.<end>"

## 1630112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これは！<line-break>本隊はいったい！？<end>"
EN: "W-what!? What happened to the main force!?<end>"
KO: "이, 이건! 본대는 대체!?<end>"

## 1630113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>！！<line-break>お主もこの戦いに参加しておったか。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! So you joined this battle as well.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>!! 너도 이 싸움에 참가하고 있었나.<end>"

## 1630114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしより先にここにたどり着くとは。<line-break>なかなかやりおるわ。<end>"
EN: "You made it here before me. Not bad at all.<end>"
KO: "나보다 먼저 여기까지 도착하다니. 제법이구나.<end>"

## 1630115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>！！<line-break>お主もこの戦いに参加しておったか。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! So you joined this battle as well.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>!! 너도 이 싸움에 참가하고 있었나.<end>"

## 1630116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、これで<line-break>この戦いの勝ちは決まったわ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Our victory is assured now.<end>"
KO: "흠, 이걸로 이 싸움의 승패는 결정됐군.<end>"

## 1630117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、次の戦功者…、<line-break><value:$28>！　入れ！<end>"
EN: "Now for the next to be honored... <value:$28>! Enter!<end>"
KO: "그럼 다음 전공자… <value:$28>! 들어오라!<end>"

## 1630118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、よくやった。<line-break>お主が最高殊勲者のひとりじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. You are among those who have earned the highest distinction!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘했다. 네가 최고 공훈자 중 한 명이다!<end>"

## 1630119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍から次の指令が下った。<line-break>周辺住民から物資の調達を行え、　　　　　<line-break>とのことだ。<end>"
EN: "The general has issued our next order: procure supplies from the local population.<end>"
KO: "장군께서 다음 지령을 내리셨다. 주변 주민에게서 물자를 조달하라는 명령이다.<end>"

## 1630120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかにも略奪行為だが、<line-break>我が軍は補給線を確保していない。<line-break>戦い続けるには物資が不足している。<line-break>王都に残っている敵が動く前に<line-break>体勢をととのえておかねば<line-break>ならないのだ。<end>"
EN: "It is plunder, plain and simple. But we have no secure supply line, and we lack the provisions to continue fighting. We must be fully prepared before the enemy remaining in the royal capital makes a move.<end>"
KO: "분명 약탈이지만 우리 군은 보급선을 확보하지 못했다. 계속 싸우려면 물자가 부족해. 왕도에 남은 적이 움직이기 전에 태세를 정비해야 한다.<end>"

## 1630121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "略奪は許されていいものではない。<line-break>だが、将軍は、この戦いを可能な限り、<line-break>早く終わらせるおつもりだ。<line-break>一気に王都を落とすための準備と<line-break>心得てほしい。<end>"
EN: "Plunder cannot be condoned. But the general means to end this war as quickly as possible. Consider this preparation for taking the royal capital in one decisive assault.<end>"
KO: "약탈은 용납될 수 없다. 하지만 장군은 이 싸움을 가능한 한 빨리 끝낼 생각이다. 단숨에 왕도를 함락하기 위한 준비라고 이해해 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1630122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々傭兵部隊は最後に出発する。　　　　　<line-break>準備をととのえておけ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Our mercenary unit will depart last. Make ready.<end>"
KO: "우리 용병부대는 마지막에 출발한다. 준비를 갖춰 둬라.<end>"

## 1630123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、敵襲！<end>"
EN: "E-enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "저, 적습이다!<end>"

## 1630124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵だと！？<line-break>どういうことだ！<end>"
EN: "The enemy!? What is going on!?<end>"
KO: "적이라고!? 무슨 소리야!<end>"

## 1630125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵部隊、出撃だ！<line-break>アンギルダン様をお救いするのだ！！<end>"
EN: "Mercenary unit, move out! We must save Lord Angeerdan!<end>"
KO: "용병 부대, 출격이다! 앙길단 님을 구해라!!<end>"

## 1630126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍！　ご無事でしたか。<end>"
EN: "General! You're safe!<end>"
KO: "장군! 무사하셨습니까.<end>"

## 1630127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、よいところに来た。<line-break>全軍に伝えよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, excellent timing. Send word to the entire army.<end>"
KO: "오, 마침 잘 왔다. 전군에 전해라.<end>"

## 1630128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "撤退じゃ！　散れ！<line-break>ドワーフの王国を抜け、<line-break>生きて帰るのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Break formation! Get out of the kingdom of the Dwarves and make it home alive!<end>"
KO: "퇴각이다! 흩어져라! 드워프 왕국을 빠져나가 살아서 돌아가라!<end>"

## 1630129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！　生きて会おうぞ！<line-break>エンシャントの政庁の前で待っておる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! We'll meet again―alive! I'll be waiting in front of the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 살아서 만나자! 엔샨트 정청 앞에서 기다리고 있겠다.<end>"

## 1630130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発隊の出撃で手薄だった<line-break>本陣を突かれた帝国軍は、<line-break>敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、総崩れとなった。<end>"
EN: "With its main camp left thinly defended by the deployment of the requisitioning force, the Imperial army was thrown into a complete rout by a surprise attack from Xenetes, who had rallied the defeated remnants with astonishing speed.<end>"
KO: "징발대 출격으로 본진이 허술해진 제국군은 패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 완전히 무너졌다.<end>"

## 1630131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生き残ったディンガル兵は、<line-break>ドワーフの王国を抜け、<line-break>ディンガル領内に撤退。<end>"
EN: "The surviving Dyneskal soldiers withdrew through the Dwarf kingdom into Dyneskal territory.<end>"
KO: "살아남은 딩갈 병사들은 드워프 왕국을 빠져나가 딩갈 영내로 철수했다.<end>"

## 1630132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスは、あえて追撃せず、<line-break>ここにアンギルダンの山越えに始まった<line-break>ロストール侵攻戦は、失敗に終わった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes deliberately chose not to pursue them, and thus the invasion of Rostorl, which began with Angeerdan's crossing of the mountains, ended in failure.<end>"
KO: "제네테스는 굳이 추격하지 않았고, 이렇게 앙길단의 산맥 돌파로 시작된 로스톨 침공전은 실패로 끝났다.<end>"

## 1630133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで、ゼネテスの声望は<line-break>一気に上がり、奇跡の名将と呼ばれたが　　<line-break>ロストールの被害は甚大なものがあった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared after this battle, and he was hailed as a miracle-working commander. Yet the damage Rostorl suffered was immense.<end>"
KO: "이 전투로 제네테스의 명망은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불렸지만, 로스톨이 입은 피해도 막대했다.<end>"

## 1630134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、<line-break>アンギルダンが討ち取られると、<line-break>帝国軍は総崩れとなった。<end>"
EN: "When Angeerdan was slain in a surprise attack by Xenetes, who had rallied the defeated soldiers with astonishing speed, the Imperial Army collapsed into a total rout.<end>"
KO: "패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 완전히 무너졌다.<end>"

## 1630135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　生き残ったディンガル兵は、　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　ドワーフの王国を抜け、<line-break>　　　　　　ディンガル領内に撤退。<end>"
EN: "The surviving Dyneskal soldiers withdrew through the Dwarf kingdom into Dyneskal territory.<end>"
KO: "살아남은 딩갈 병사들은 드워프 왕국을 빠져나가 딩갈 영내로 철수했다.<end>"
