## 1630138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。それならば致し方ない。<line-break>立ち去るがよい。我々は大兵力だ。<line-break>情報が漏れても負けることはない。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then so be it. You may go. Our army is vast; even if word gets out, we cannot lose.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없지. 물러가라. 우리는 대병력이다. 정보가 새도 질 일은 없다.<end>"

## 1630139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デバッグ用ですー。<line-break>消去忘れないでくださいねー。<end>"
EN: "This is for debugging. Don't forget to delete it, okay?<end>"
KO: "디버그용입니다~. 지우는 거 잊지 마세요~.<end>"

## 1630140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec163-part99.toml

EN: "This shield is the token of your knighthood. Take it.<end>"
KO: "이건 기사의 증표인 방패다. 받아라.<end>"

## 1640001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、<value:$28>か。<line-break>いくつか仕事もこなして、<line-break>冒険者らしくなってきたな。　　<line-break>リベルダムには、行ったか？<line-break>あそこは冒険者ギルドの本部だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You've handled a few jobs now. You're starting to look like a real adventurer. Have you been to Liberdam? That's where the Adventurers' Guild headquarters is.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 몇 가지 일도 해내고 모험자다워졌군. 리벨덤에는 가봤나? 거긴 모험자 길드 본부다.<end>"

## 1640002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ一度行って、　　　　　<line-break>顔を売っておけ。<line-break>あいさつがてらにな。<line-break>あ、そん時は気をつけろよ。<line-break>なんでも、冒険者を狙ってる<line-break>エルフがいるって話だからな。<end>"
EN: "You should head over there soon, introduce yourself, and start making a name for yourself. Just be careful when you do. I hear there's an Elf targeting adventurers.<end>"
KO: "슬슬 한번 가서 얼굴을 알려 둬. 인사도 할 겸. 아, 그때는 조심해. 모험자를 노리는 엘프가 있다는 얘기니까.<end>"

## 1640003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変わったことですか？<line-break>それも魔族とか魔物とか<line-break>そんなたぐいの奴ねえ…。<end>"
EN: "Anything unusual? Demons, monsters, that sort of thing...?<end>"
KO: "이상한 일 말인가요? 그것도 마족이나 마물 같은 종류의 녀석 말이죠….<end>"

## 1640004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。<line-break>ささいなことでもかまわない。<line-break>　<line-break>闇の気配を感じるような話を<line-break>聞いていたら、教えてほしい。<end>"
EN: "Yes. No matter how trivial it may seem. If you've heard anything that suggests the presence of darkness, tell me.<end>"
KO: "그래. 사소한 것이라도 상관없다. 어둠의 기운이 느껴지는 이야기를 들었다면 알려 줘.<end>"

## 1640005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そういえば、<line-break>ここんとこ街を騒がせている<line-break>エルフがいますよ。<line-break>ダークエルフなんですが、<line-break>冒険者を狩ってましてね。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, there's an Elf who's been causing trouble around town lately. A Dark Elf. She's been hunting adventurers.<end>"
KO: "…그러고 보니 요즘 도시를 떠들썩하게 하는 엘프가 있습니다. 다크엘프인데 모험자들을 사냥하고 있죠.<end>"

## 1640006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダークエルフ…。<end>"
EN: "A Dark Elf...<end>"
KO: "다크 엘프….<end>"

## 1640007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けっこうな手練ですよ。<line-break>名をあげようと挑んだ冒険者は<line-break>全員やられちまってます。<end>"
EN: "She's quite skilled. Every adventurer who's challenged her hoping to make a name has been beaten.<end>"
KO: "제법 솜씨 있는 녀석입니다. 이름을 날리려고 도전한 모험자들은 전부 당했어요.<end>"

## 1640008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんなところに出くわすとは<line-break>運がいいな。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. What luck, running into you here.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 이런 데서 마주치다니 운이 좋군.<end>"

## 1640009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼があのネメアだ。<line-break>冒険者にとっちゃ神様のような人だ。<line-break>知ってるだろ？<end>"
EN: "That's Nemea. He's practically a god to adventurers. You've heard of him, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "저 사람이 그 네메아야. 모험자한테는 신 같은 사람이지. 알고 있지?<end>"

## 1640010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>というのか。<line-break>かけ出しの冒険者のようだな。<end>"
EN: "So your name is <value:$28>. You seem new to adventuring.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이라고 하나. 이제 막 시작한 모험자 같군.<end>"

## 1640011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、<line-break>…いい目をしている。<end>"
EN: "But... there's a good look in your eyes.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 좋은 눈을 하고 있군.<end>"

## 1640012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダークエルフだ！<line-break>ダークエルフが出たぞ！<end>"
EN: "The Dark Elf! The Dark Elf's here!<end>"
KO: "다크 엘프다! 다크 엘프가 나타났다!<end>"

## 1640013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "捕まえろ！　殺せ！<end>"
EN: "Catch her! Kill her!<end>"
KO: "잡아라! 죽여라!<end>"

## 1640014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、出やがったか！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, she's back!<end>"
KO: "또 나타났냐!<end>"

## 1640015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かなり近いな。<line-break>行ってみるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "That was close by. Let us see what's happening.<end>"
KO: "꽤 가깝군. 가 보자.<end>"

## 1640016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この事件も一件落着だな。<line-break>噂のダークエルフも<line-break>あの人にゃ、かなうまい。<end>"
EN: "That settles this whole affair. Even the Dark Elf we've heard so much about would be no match for that person.<end>"
KO: "이 사건도 일단락됐군. 소문의 다크엘프도 저 사람을 이기진 못하겠지.<end>"

## 1640017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "騒ぎが収まるまで<line-break>ここでじっとしておった方が<line-break>いいぞ。<end>"
EN: "You'd best stay put here until the commotion dies down.<end>"
KO: "소란이 가라앉을 때까지 여기서 가만히 있는 게 좋다.<end>"

## 1640018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっと言う間でした。<line-break>どこから撃ってきたのかさえ<line-break>わかりませんでしたよ。<end>"
EN: "It was over in an instant. I couldn't even tell where the shot came from.<end>"
KO: "순식간이었습니다. 어디서 쏜 건지조차 알 수 없었어요.<end>"

## 1640019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんちゅうスゴ腕だ。<line-break>ダークエルフは恐いのう。<end>"
EN: "Now that's some skill. Dark Elves are a frightening lot.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 실력이군. 다크엘프는 무섭구나.<end>"

## 1640020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間に合わなかった。<line-break>…矢で急所を一撃だ。<end>"
EN: "Too late. A single arrow to a vital spot.<end>"
KO: "늦었어. …화살 한 발에 급소를 맞았어.<end>"

## 1640021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたぞ！　スラムだ！<line-break>スラムへ逃げ込んだぞ！<end>"
EN: "There! The Slums! Our quarry fled into the Slums!<end>"
KO: "찾았다! 슬럼가다! 슬럼가로 도망쳤다!<end>"

## 1640022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "Time to go.<end>"
KO: "간다.<end>"

## 1640023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…殺してやる！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前たちが妹を殺した！<line-break>絶対に、許さない！<end>"
EN: "I'll kill you! You killed my sister! I'll never forgive you!<end>"
KO: "…죽여 주마! 네놈들이 여동생을 죽였어! 절대 용서 못 해!<end>"

## 1640024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！<line-break>こいつは俺たちの獲物だ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! She's our prey!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 이건 우리 사냥감이다!<end>"

## 1640025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まさか…ネメア…！<end>"
EN: "It... it can't be. Nemea...!<end>"
KO: "서, 설마… 네메아…!<end>"

## 1640026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、この人が！？<end>"
EN: "Th-this is him!?<end>"
KO: "이, 이 사람이!?<end>"

## 1640027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…魔王バロルを倒した英雄。<line-break>こんなところで出会えるとは…。<end>"
EN: "The hero who defeated Balor, the Demon King... To think we'd meet him here...<end>"
KO: "…마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 영웅. 이런 곳에서 만나게 될 줄이야….<end>"

## 1640028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "下がっていろ。<end>"
EN: "Stay back.<end>"
KO: "물러서 있어.<end>"

## 1640029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様もこいつらの仲間か！<line-break>私の妹を殺した冒険者の仲間か！！<end>"
EN: "Are you with them too? Are you an ally of the adventurers who killed my sister!?<end>"
KO: "네놈도 이 녀석들 동료냐! 내 여동생을 죽인 모험자들의 동료냐!!<end>"

## 1640030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか……。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>冒険者に妹を殺され、<line-break>その怒りと憎しみで<line-break>闇にむしばまれたか。<end>"
EN: "I see... Adventurers killed your sister, and rage and hatred have left you consumed by darkness.<end>"
KO: "그런가……. 모험자에게 여동생을 잃고 그 분노와 증오로 어둠에 잠식된 건가.<end>"

## 1640031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様らの仲間に…<line-break>冒険者に殺された。<line-break>　<line-break>さんざん痛めつけられて…<line-break>いたぶられて…<line-break>妹は…死んだ…。<end>"
EN: "Your comrades... the adventurers killed her. They beat her mercilessly... tormented her... and my sister... died...<end>"
KO: "네놈들 동료에게… 모험자에게 죽었다. 실컷 고통받고… 괴롭힘당하고… 내 여동생은… 죽었어….<end>"

## 1640032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…許さない！<line-break>貴様らを許さない！<end>"
EN: "I won't forgive you! I will never forgive any of you!<end>"
KO: "…용서 못 해! 네놈들을 용서 못 해!<end>"

## 1640033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構えろ、<value:$28>。<line-break>彼女は今、周りが見えていない。<line-break>見境なく攻撃してくるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ready yourself, <value:$28>. She can't see what's around her right now. She'll attack indiscriminately.<end>"
KO: "자세를 잡아, <value:$28>. 지금 그녀는 주변이 보이지 않아. 닥치는 대로 공격할 거다.<end>"

## 1640034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死ね！<end>"
EN: "Die!<end>"
KO: "죽어!<end>"

## 1640035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やった！<end>"
EN: "We did it!<end>"
KO: "해냈다!<end>"

## 1640036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このダークエルフ、<line-break>どうするつもりです？<end>"
EN: "What are you going to do with this Dark Elf?<end>"
KO: "이 다크 엘프, 어떻게 할 생각입니까?<end>"

## 1640037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺す気はない。<line-break>私に任せてほしい。<end>"
EN: "I have no intention of killing her. Leave her to me.<end>"
KO: "죽일 생각은 없어. 나에게 맡겨 줘.<end>"

## 1640038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わ、わかりました。<end>"
EN: "V-very well.<end>"
KO: "…아, 알겠습니다.<end>"

## 1640039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、帰るぞ。<end>"
EN: "Come on. We're leaving.<end>"
KO: "야, 돌아가자.<end>"

## 1640040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、けどよ。<end>"
EN: "What? But―<end>"
KO: "어, 하지만.<end>"

## 1640041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ野郎！　さっきの戦いを見ただろ。<line-break>このダークエルフと俺たちが戦っていたら、<line-break>俺たちは死んでいた。<line-break>俺たちはネメアさんに命を救われたんだ。<line-break>ここはネメアさんに任せて、退くんだ。<end>"
EN: "You fool! You saw that fight. If we'd fought this Dark Elf ourselves, we'd be dead. Nemea saved our lives. We're leaving her to him and getting out of here.<end>"
KO: "바보 자식! 방금 싸움 봤잖아. 이 다크엘프와 우리가 싸웠다면 우린 죽었어. 우리는 네메아 씨에게 목숨을 구원받은 거야. 여긴 네메아 씨에게 맡기고 물러난다.<end>"

## 1640042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わかったよ。<end>"
EN: "A-all right.<end>"
KO: "아, 알았어.<end>"

## 1640043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "情けをかけるつもりか！？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…殺せ！<end>"
EN: "Do you mean to show me mercy!? Then... kill me!<end>"
KO: "자비를 베풀 셈이냐!? …죽여라!<end>"

## 1640044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げるのか。<end>"

## 1640046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1640047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前に何がわかる！<line-break>憎しみで闇に染まったエルフに<line-break>生きる場所などあるものか！<end>"
EN: "What could you possibly know? There is no place in this world for an Elf stained by the darkness of hatred!<end>"
KO: "네가 뭘 알아! 증오로 어둠에 물든 엘프에게 살아갈 곳 따위 있을 것 같아!<end>"

## 1640048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心まで闇に負けたわけではあるまい。<line-break>…急ぐことはない。<line-break>死ぬのは簡単だ。<line-break>その前に考えろ。<line-break>生きているお前が<line-break>死んだ妹のためにできることを。<end>"
EN: "The darkness has not conquered your heart. There is no need to hurry. Dying is easy. Before you choose it, consider what you can do for your dead sister while you still live.<end>"
KO: "마음까지 어둠에 진 건 아니겠지. 서두를 필요 없다. 죽는 건 쉽다. 그 전에 생각해라. 살아 있는 네가 죽은 여동생을 위해 할 수 있는 일을.<end>"

## 1640049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹のために？<end>"
EN: "For my sister?<end>"
KO: "여동생을 위해서?<end>"

## 1640050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…仇を討つ。<line-break>それ以外、何ができる！<end>"
EN: "I'll avenge her. What else can I do!?<end>"
KO: "…원수를 갚는다. 그 밖에 뭘 할 수 있겠어!<end>"

## 1640051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "答えは自分で探せ。<line-break>人に求めて得られるものではない。<line-break>　<line-break>生きて、そして、<line-break>自分で見つけるのだ。<end>"
EN: "You must seek the answer yourself. No one else can give it to you. Live, and find it for yourself.<end>"
KO: "답은 스스로 찾아라. 남에게서 구해 얻을 수 있는 게 아니다. 살아서, 그리고 직접 찾아내는 거다.<end>"

## 1640052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…また会うこともあろう。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps we shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "…다시 만날 일도 있겠지.<end>"

## 1640053

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1640054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml

## 1640062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹をあんな目にあわせて、<line-break>私の体を憎しみに染めて、<line-break>それでも生きろだなんて…。<end>"

## 1640063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>冒険者に腕試しを挑んでくる　<line-break>ボルダンには会ったかい？<line-break>あちこちの街道に出没して、<line-break>強そうな冒険者に<line-break>勝負を挑んでくるらしいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Have you run into Boldan, the one who challenges adventurers to tests of skill? Apparently, Boldan shows up on roads all over the place, challenging any adventurer who looks strong.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 모험자에게 실력 겨루기를 거는 볼단을 만났어? 여러 가도에 나타나 강해 보이는 모험자에게 승부를 건다더군.<end>"

## 1640064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前もそろそろ危ないぞ。<line-break>いっぱしの冒険者に<line-break>見えるようになってきたからな。　<line-break>まあ、殺されないし、<line-break>何か盗られるわけでもない。　　<end>"
EN: "You'd better watch out yourself. You're starting to look like a proper adventurer. Still, you won't be killed, and nothing will be stolen from you.<end>"
KO: "너도 슬슬 위험하겠군. 제법 모험자처럼 보이기 시작했으니까. 뭐, 죽지는 않고 뭔가 빼앗기는 것도 아니지만.<end>"

## 1640065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出会っちまったら<line-break>力試しだと思って、戦ってみな。<line-break>しかし、まったく迷惑な奴だよ。<end>"
EN: "If you run into Boldan, treat it as a chance to test your strength and take up the challenge. Still, what a nuisance.<end>"
KO: "마주치면 실력 시험이라 생각하고 싸워 봐. 정말 민폐인 녀석이야.<end>"

## 1640066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You there, adventurer. Halt.<end>"
KO: "거기 모험자, 기다려.<end>"

## 1640067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1640070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>覚えておく。<line-break>この敗北を胸に、自らを鍛え直す。<line-break>心して待つがいい。<line-break>次は、負けぬ。<end>"

## 1640071

## 1640077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1640083

## 1640084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec164-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らボルダンの中でも<line-break>最強の戦士レーグは、<line-break>お前と引き分けたと聞く。<line-break>ならば我は、<line-break>いずれお前を倒し、<line-break>レーグをも超えて見せよう。<end>"

## 1640085

## 1640091

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのボルダンを追っていたのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>手当たりしだいに冒険者を襲う<line-break>ボルダンと聞いて、<line-break>闇の手の者かと思ってな。<end>"

## 1640092

## 1640093

## 1680001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…レムオンをかばうとは。<line-break>ゼネテスには自棄なところがあったが<line-break>そなたも、自滅型だったとはな。<line-break>覚えておくがよい。<line-break>手負いの鷹は、冷静なときよりも<line-break>危険だということを。<end>"
EN: "So, you chose to protect Lemghon. Xenetes always had a self-destructive streak, but it seems you do as well. Remember this: a wounded hawk is more dangerous than one that has kept its composure.<end>"
KO: "…레무온을 감싸다니. 제네테스에게 자포자기한 면이 있었지만 그대도 자멸형이었군. 기억해 둬라. 상처 입은 매는 냉정할 때보다 위험하다는 것을.<end>"

## 1680002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうなれば、いた仕方ない。<line-break>できる限りの手を打つまでだ。<line-break>ただ、ティアナの身が案じられる。<end>"
EN: "It has come to this, then. We must do everything we can. Still, I fear for Tiana's safety.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상 어쩔 수 없다. 할 수 있는 수단은 모두 쓰겠다. 다만 티아나의 신변이 걱정되는군.<end>"

## 1680004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先の空中庭園のおり…<line-break>そなた、気づいたか？<line-break>…アトレイアだ。<line-break>あのときの<line-break>アトレイアの表情には<line-break>鬼気迫るものがあった。<end>"
EN: "When we were last at the Hanging Gardens... did you notice? I mean Atleia. There was something frighteningly intense in her expression.<end>"
KO: "지난 공중정원 때… 자네도 눈치챘나? …아트레이아 말일세. 그때 아트레이아의 표정에는 섬뜩할 만큼 절박한 기운이 있었어.<end>"

## 1680005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの娘の人生には<line-break>少し、関わりがあってな。<line-break>気になるのだ…。<end>"
EN: "I have had some small part in that girl's life. Naturally, I am concerned.<end>"
KO: "그 아이의 인생에는 조금 연관이 있어서 말이지. 신경이 쓰이는군….<end>"

## 1680006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>ティアナを頼むぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Take care of Tiana for me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 티아나를 부탁한다.<end>"
